2026-10-23 21:50:22.187 
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187 The Arduino sketch probes BQ on the closed-bus baseline, then opens
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187 TCA channel 2 from its own master, and reports whether BQ ACKs in
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187 each state. Expected outcomes:
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187   - BQ NACKs Arduino on TCA2 -> Story D-revised confirmed
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187   - BQ ACKs Arduino on TCA2  -> Story F confirmed
2026-10-23 21:50:22.187

2026-10-23 21:50:22.187 is still alive while the Arduino has the bus. Press RESET to exit.
